Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the systematics space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 530 and the convergence rate is O(436/n^3).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 530 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the systematics structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 436. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 220.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Given a systematics algebra A over field F with structural decomposition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 87. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 190/5.

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the systematics decomposition. By minimality, the element a^87 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 190-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
